2. Fourier/modulation full-length:
   Start from best pattern candidate (or all ones). Use iterative subgradient/multiplicative updates for full p. Minimize surrogate max convolution. Since objective scale invariant, normalize p sum=1 or sqrt(2n). We can maintain p nonnegative. Need avoid all-ones stationary.

Subgradient update for active lags:
- Compute c = linear conv p via FFT (surrogate), maybe normalize.
- Let c_max = max(c). Active lags where c > c_max*(1-tol) maybe few. Gradient g = zeros; for each active lag i, add p reversed segment: g[j] += p[i-j] if valid. But constructing g O(n * num_active). If many active, O(n^2). Need sparse: choose top few lags (e.g., 5) to limit.
- Update p = p * exp(-alpha * g) (multiplicative weights), clip >=0, normalize.
This is like interior point. Starting from tiled pattern (not all ones) may work. But gradient of max at center is p reversed, which if p is periodic may cause changes.

Alternatively use projected gradient: p ← p - alpha*g, renormalize, clip. Multiplicative ensures nonneg.

Need choose alpha small. Could also add random perturbations.

def full_update(a, eta, top):
    c = conv_max_details(a)  # need full c? To get active lags, need c array length 2n-1. FFT returns O(n), okay.
    c_scale = c.max()
    active = np.nonzero(c > 0.95*c_scale)[0]  # maybe many (O(n)?). For triangular all ones, active around center one? For flat random maybe many. Limit to top K using argpartition.
    if len(active)>K: active = argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    g = zeros(n)
    for i in active:
        jmin = max(0, i-(n-1)); jmax = min(n-1,i)
        # g[j] += a[i-j]
        # a segment reversed: a[i-j] for j in [jmin,jmax] = a[i-jmin : i-jmax+1][::-1]
        g[jmin:jmax+1] += a[i-jmax : i-jmin+1][::-1]? Need indexes carefully.
```
Given i, valid j where 0<=j<n and 0<=i-j<n => j in [max(0,i-n+1), min(i,n-1)]. Need g[j] += a[i-j]. The array of a indices from i-jmin down to i-jmax. So `gseg = a[i-jmax : i-jmin+1][::-1]`, length = jmax-jmin+1. Add to g slice. For K small (5 or 20) O(Kn).

```
def binary_pattern_search(m, deadline):
    total = 1 << m
    if m > 20: total = 1 << 20? no
    counts = [0]*(2*m-1)
    mask = 0
    best_score = np.inf
    best_mask = (1 << m) - 1
    # maybe evaluate all ones initially? We can let loop.
    for step in range(1, total):
        b = (step & -step).bit_length() - 1
        toggling_on = not (mask >> b) & 1
        if toggling_on:
            counts[2*b] += 1
            mm = mask
            while mm:
                lsb = mm & -mm
                j = lsb.bit_length() - 1
                counts[b+j] += 2
                mm -= lsb
        else:
            counts[2*b] -= 1
            mm = mask ^ (1 << b)  # other bits? If mask has b set, other = mask without b
            while mm:
                lsb = mm & -mm
                j = lsb.bit_length() - 1
                counts[b+j] -= 2
                mm -= lsb
        mask ^= (1 << b)
        k = mask.bit_count()
        if k >= 2:
            # optional skip if lower bound > best: max at least ceil(k^2/(2m-1)); score min possible. Not necessary.
            mx = max(counts)
            score = 2.0*m*mx/(k*k)
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_mask = mask
        if (step & 4095) == 0 and time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
    return best_mask, best_score
```

```
def improve_pattern(p, rng, iterations, deadline):
    m = len(p)
    p = np.asarray(p, float).copy()
    p = np.clip(p, 0, None)
    s = p.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return p, inf
    p = (p/s)*m  # sum m
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = pattern_score_direct(p)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    while it < iterations and time.monotonic()<deadline:
        it +=1
        c = np.convolve(p,p)
        # select top active
        K = min(3, c.size)
        if c.size <= K: active = np.argsort(c)[-K:]
        else: active = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        g = np.zeros(m)
        for i in active:
            jmin = max(0, i-(m-1)); jmax = min(m-1,i)
            g[jmin:jmax+1] += p[i-jmax:i-jmin+1][::-1]  # check
        # multiplicative gradient
        if g.max() > 0:
            q1 = p * np.exp(-0.3 * g / g.max())
            q1 = np.clip(q1, 0, None)
            if q1.sum() > 0: q1 = (q1/q1.sum())*m
            # evaluate
        q2 = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.2, m)); clip norm
        q3 = p.copy(); choose random pos set 0 or random weight.
        for q in variants: if finite and score < best_score - 1e-12: accept.
        if no_improve > 20: break
    return best_p, best_score
```

Maybe we can optimize periodic patterns with gradient. Let's design robust pattern optimizer.

Since m small, we can use a general non-convex optimization heuristic:
- Parameterize p as softmax of real vector z shifted to ensure nonnegative? Softmax p = exp(z - max)/sum. Sum can be normalized. This avoids clipping. But p can be very small. We can do coordinate descent/random coordinate gradient of f≈max c.
- f = 2m max(c)/(sum p)^2. Normalize sum=1 after each update, f=2m max(c). Minimize max(c) over simplex.
- Subgradient of max(c): active set A where c_i near max, gradient g = sum_{i∈A} ∇c_i (or average). ∇c_i[p_j]=p_{i-j} for valid j (with coefficient? c_i = p_j p_{i-j}. If i-j = j? derivative? Let's handle diagonal: c_i = sum_j p_j p_{i-j}. Derivative wrt p_k = p_{i-k} + p_k if k = i-k (i even)?? Because term p_j p_{i-j}: derivative p_k: from j=k gives p_{i-k}; from i-j=k (j=i-k) gives p_k, if k != i-k distinct; both same if k=i-k (i even) then derivative 2? Actually if i=2k, term p_k*p_k appears once in sum? c_i = sum_j p_j p_{i-j}. When i=2k, j=k term p_k p_k, derivative 2p_k. Also the two contributions formula p_{i-k}+p_k gives 2p_k. So derivative g_j = p_{i-j} + δ_{j,i-j} p_j? But p_{i-j}=p_j when equal, so g_j = p_{i-j} + 1_{2j=i} p_j. Equivalently for active i, add vector v_i[j] = p[(i-j) %? valid] plus double if 2j=i. But for our previous gradient of c_i as sum over valid j, we used p[i-j]; that was wrong for diagonal because did not double. Does it matter? It's a valid subgradient? Since c_i is not differentiable at diagonal? Actually c_i is a polynomial, differentiable. The derivative includes double on diagonal. For nonnegative, the half gradient maybe still descent? We should use correct for better.
But note for convolution polynomial P^2 coefficient c_i = ∑_{j+k=i} p_j p_k. If j≠k, derivative wrt p_l includes p_{i-l}; if l = i/2, term appears p_l^2 derivative 2p_l. So active gradient `v[l] = p[i-l]` for valid, and if 2l==i then add p[i-l] again.
- Since p normalized sum=1, after subgradient projection: p <- p - η * (g - mean(g)) maybe, then project to simplex (clip negative, renormalize). Multiplicative update p <- p * exp(-η(g - mean?)) and normalize. Multiplicative handles nonneg, no projection. Use g normalized.
- Active set: choose top few c_i. Starting from random non-palindromic can descend.

We can implement pattern optimization for m values, with multiple random starts, using multiplicative subgradient.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_pattern_simple(m, rng, iters, deadline):
    best_score=inf; best_p=zeros
    for restart in range(restarts):
       if rng.random()<0.5: p = binary_mask_to_p(best_mask?) else random Dirichlet
       p = p/p.sum()
       no_improve=0
       for it in range(iters):
          c = np.convolve(p,p)
          cp = c.max()
          score = 2*m*cp  # since sum=1, pattern score = 2m cp. Wait pattern score formula 2m max/S^2, sum=1 -> 2m max.
          if score < best_score: update.
          # active: top K where c > cp- tol
          tol = 1e-6 + 1e-3*cp
          active = np.nonzero(c >= cp - tol)[0]
          if active.size==0: active=[argmax]
          if active.size > 8: active=top 8
          g = zeros(m)
          for i in active:
             c_i = 1.0/active.size
             jmin = max(0, i-m+1); jmax=min(m-1,i)
             v = p[i-jmax:i-jmin+1][::-1] * c_i
             g[jmin:jmax+1] += v
             mid = i/2
             if i%2==0 and 0<=mid<m and jmin<=mid<=jmax: g[mid] += p[mid]*c_i
          # normalize g for scaling
          gmax = g.max()
          if gmax <=0: break
          g = g / gmax
          # multiplicative update with learning rate
          eta = 0.5 # or decays
          q = p * np.exp(-eta * (g - g.mean()))
          q = np.clip(q, 1e-12, None)
          q = q/q.sum()
          # occasionally randomize
          if it % 10 == 0:
              mask = rng.random(m) < 0.1
              q = q * np.where(mask, np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,m)), 1)
              q = q/q.sum()
          qscore = pattern_score_sum1(q)
          if qscore < score - 1e-12:
              p=q; score=qscore; no_improve=0
          else:
              no_improve +=1
              eta *=0.8
          if no_improve>30: break
    return best_p,best_score
```

Let's compute general two-block c more carefully and optimize.

Sequence: indices 0..A-1 value a, A..A+B-1 value b. n=A+B.
Coefficient c_i.
For i < A: c_i = (i+1) a^2.
For A <= i < A+B-1: cross plus maybe a-a/b-b? Let's derive cases.
Let a positions [0,A-1], b [A,A+B-1].
For sum i:
- a-a: count_AA(i) = number j in [0,A-1] with i-j in [0,A-1]. This is nonzero for i=0..2A-2. Count = min(i+1, 2A-1-i, A).
- b-b: shift by 2A: nonzero for i=2A..2A+2B-2. Count_BB(i-2A) = min(i-2A+1, 2B-1-(i-2A), B).
- cross a-b: i between A and A+B-2: count_AC(i) = #j in [0,A-1] with i-j in [A,A+B-1] = #j in [i-A-B+1, i-A] ∩ [0,A-1]. count_BA same. Total cross =2*count_AC.
The combination of a-a and cross or b-b can overlap if A and B not separated enough. In our equal-peak design A=n/5,B=4n/5, A+B=n, 2A=2n/5, so a-a support 0..2A-2=0..0.4n-2, cross A..n-2=0.2n..0.8n-2, b-b 2A..? 0.4n..? Overlap: a-a and cross overlap for i from A to 2A-2 (0.2n to0.4n), cross and b-b overlap for i=2A to n-2 (0.4n to0.8n). Thus peaks can add.

We can optimize A,B,a,b to flatten including overlaps. For large n, scale continuous variables. Let A=α n, B=(1-α)n. Normalize sum=1: a = S_a? Let density u=a n (so total mass α u), v=b n (mass (1-α)v), α u+(1-α)v=1. Coefficients scale as 1/n times functions. Score =2 * peak_function (since max ~ P/n). Need minimize P.

def propose_candidate(...):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   deadline = time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s)-5
   parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=float).clip(0,1000)

   if invalid: fallback ones?
   best=parent.copy(); best_score=evaluate...
   candidates=[]

       p=mask_to_p; maybe continuous optimize p for pattern length m.

       if time remains and n>10:
          start from best (normalized)

          current = best.copy(); normalize sum1
          cur_surr = 2*n*_conv_max(current,N)
          best_surr=cur_surr; best_seq=current.copy()
          eta=0.3; noimp=0
          while time<deadline-50:

              qmax=...

def _convolve_fft(a, N):
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irf(f*f, N)
    return c[:2*a.size-1]
```

for i in idx:

    jhi = min(i, n - 1)
    # indices a[i - jlo : i - jhi + 1]

    if i % 2 == 0:
        mid = i//2
        if jlo <= mid <= jhi:
            g[mid] += a[mid]
```

```
def _optimize_pattern(p, rng, max_steps, deadline):
    m = len(p); p = p.copy(); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return p, inf
    p = p/s
    best = p.copy(); best_f = 2*m*np.convolve(p,p).max()
    eta=0.5
    no=0
    for step in range(max_steps):
       if time... break
       c=np.convolve(p,p); cm=c.max(); f=2*m*cm
       if f < best_f - 1e-12: best=p.copy(); best_f=f
       # active top K small (4)
       K=min(4,c.size)
       active=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
       g=zeros; for i in active: ... (double diag)
       if g.max()>0: g/=g.max()
       q=p*np.exp(-eta*g)  # perhaps no centering
       q=np.clip(q,1e-12,None); q/=q.sum()
       # random restart if no improve: q = mix with random dirichlet
       qf = 2*m*np.convolve(q,q).max()
       if qf < f:
           p=q; f=qf; no=0; eta=min(1.2,eta*1.05)
       else:
           no+=1; eta*=0.7
           if no>20:
               p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,m)); p=np.clip(...); p/=p.sum(); no=0
       if no>100: break
    # also evaluate p*flipped? pattern reversal same score? no need.
    return best,best_f
```

Let's design final code carefully.

Top-level helpers without lambdas:
- `def _as_1d(a):` maybe.
- `def _exact(seq):` call evaluate_sequence. Need list; if seq is np array, convert.
- `def _tile(pattern, n):` if n<=0 return np.ones(1). Pattern np array. `m=len(pattern); reps=n//m; out=np.zeros(n); if reps>0: out[:reps*m] = np.tile(pattern, reps); return out`. If pattern length >n? reps=0 => zeros sum0 invalid. Need ensure if m>n, maybe use parent or scale pattern to n? We'll only use m<=n. If n<m, choose m=n? Pattern search m small.
- `def _pattern_objective(p):` `c=np.convolve(p,p); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-15: return inf; return 2*len(p)*c.max()/(s*s)`.
- `def _mask_pattern(mask,m):` `np.array([(mask>>i)&1 for i in range(m)], float)` but list comprehension is okay? Lambda? No nested function. List comprehension allowed? It's not a lambda. But to avoid long, okay. Or use `np.fromiter`? Simpler loop.
- `def _binary_search(m, deadline):` as above. If m > 20, maybe sample. We'll call m=13,16 maybe 18 if time.
- `def _optimize_pattern_continuous(p, rng, deadline, steps=400):` local pattern improvement. Need no nested. We can use `_pattern_objective` each eval. Active gradient. Let's implement with a tuple of variants? To avoid nested, define separate helper `_pattern_gradient(p, c, active)`.
```
def _pattern_gradient(p, c, active):
    m=len(p); g=np.zeros(m)
    for i in active:
       jlo=max(0,i-m+1); jhi=min(i,m-1)
       g[jlo:jhi+1] += p[i-jhi:i-jlo+1][::-1]
       if i%2==0:
          mid=i//2
          if jlo<=mid<=jhi: g[mid]+=p[mid]
    return g
```
- `_normalize_p(p, floor=1e-12):` clip, sum, if sum<=0 return None, divide.

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def _is_finite_pos_seq(s): ...

def _pattern_objective(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    return float(2.0 * p.size * c.max() / (s*s))

def _build_tile(pattern, n):
    n=int(n); m=len(pattern)
    out=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if m <=0 or n <=0: return out
    reps=n//m
    if reps>0:
       out[:reps*m] = np.tile(pattern, reps)
    return out

def _mask_to_pattern(mask, m):
    p=np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    for i in range(m):
       if (mask >> i) & 1: p[i]=1.0
    return p

def _binary_search(m, deadline):
    total = 1 << m
    if total > 1<<18: total = (1<<18)  # sample? but gray loop only up to total? If truncate, not exhaustive. Maybe just not call >18.
    counts=[0]*(2*m-1)
    mask=0
    best_score=np.inf
    best_mask=(1<<m)-1
    for step in range(1,total):
        b=(step & -step).bit_length()-1
        if (mask >> b) & 1:
           # remove
           counts[2*b]-=1
           mm=mask ^ (1<<b)
           while mm:
              lsb=mm & -mm; j=lsb.bit_length()-1; counts[b+j]-=2; mm-=lsb
        else:
           counts[2*b]+=1
           mm=mask
           while mm:
              lsb=mm & -mm; j=lsb.bit_length()-1; counts[b+j]+=2; mm-=lsb
        mask ^= (1<<b)
        k=mask.bit_count()
        if k:
           mx=max(counts)
           if mx:
              score=2.0*m*mx/(k*k)
              if score<best_score:
                 best_score=score; best_mask=mask
        if (step & 2047)==0 and time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
    return best_mask,best_score
```
Potential bug: In removal, `counts[2*b]-=1` uses old mask includes b. Good. In add, after update counts for other old bits; then toggle.

Continuous pattern optimize:
```
def _active_top(c, k):
    n=c.size
    if k <=0: return empty
    if k >= n: return np.arange(n)
    return np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]

def _gradient_from_active(p, active):
    m=p.size; g=np.zeros(m)
    for i in active:
       i=int(i)
       jlo = i - m + 1
       if jlo < 0: jlo=0
       jhi = i
       if jhi >= m: jhi=m-1
       if jhi < jlo: continue
       g[jlo:jhi+1] += p[i-jhi:i-jlo+1][::-1]
       if i % 2 == 0:
          mid = i//2
          if jlo <= mid <= jhi:
             g[mid] += p[mid]
    return g

def _pattern_update(p, active, eta, rng):
    g = _gradient_from_active(p, active)
    gm = float(g.max())
    if gm <= 0:
       return p.copy()
    g = g / gm
    q = p * np.exp(-eta * g)
    q = np.clip(q, 1e-12, None)
    s=q.sum()
    if s <= 0: return p.copy()
    return q/s
```
Need no lambdas.

Detailed full search implementation:
```
def _next_pow2(x):
    n=1
    while n < x: n <<= 1
    return n

def _conv_fft(a, N):
    f=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, N)
    return c[:2*a.size-1]

def _full_gradient(p, active, N? no): same as pattern gradient but full.
```
Gradient function works for any length. Active top from c.

def _full_refine(start, n, rng, deadline, max_iters):
    a = np.asarray(start,float).copy(); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: ...
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000); a /= max(1e-15,a.sum())
    N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best_surr_a=a.copy(); best_surr=2*n*_conv_fft(a,N).max()
    a_work=a.copy(); cur=best_surr; eta=0.3; no=0
    best_exact_score? not here.
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       c=_conv_fft(a_work,N); cm=c.max(); cur=2*n*cm
       if cm <=0: break
       active=_active_top(c, min(16, c.size))
       g=_gradient_from_active(a_work, active)
       if g.max()>0:
          g/=g.max()
          q=a_work*np.exp(-eta*(g - g.mean()*0.5?)) # maybe just g
          q=np.clip(q,1e-15,None); qs=q.sum(); if qs>0: q/=qs
          qcm=_conv_fft(q,N).max(); qscore=2*n*qcm
          if qscore < cur:
             if qscore < best_surr*(1-1e-6): best_surr=qscore; best_surr_a=q.copy()
             a_work=q; cur=qscore; no=0; eta=min(1.0,eta*1.03)
          else:
             # try block noise
             q2 = _block_perturb(a_work, rng)
             q2s=q2.sum(); if q2s>0: q2/=q2s
             q2cm=_conv_fft(q2,N).max()
             if 2*n*q2cm < cur:
                 a_work=q2; cur=2*n*q2cm; no=0
             else:
                 no +=1; eta*=0.85
                 if no > 50:
                    a_work = _block_perturb(a_work, rng, 0.8)
                    a_work/=a_work.sum(); no=0
             if no > 300 or eta < 1e-4: break
    return best_surr_a, best_surr
```

`_block_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.3):`
```
q=a.copy(); n=len(q)
for _ in range(6):
   width=int(np.clip(rng.integers(max(1,n//400), max(2,n//20)),1,n))
   start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))

q=np.clip(q,0,None)
s=q.sum(); if s<=0: return a
return q/s
```

```
def _optimize_pattern(p, rng, steps, deadline):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return p, float('inf')
    p = p / s
    m = p.size
    best = p.copy()
    best_f = 2.0 * float(m) * float(np.convolve(p, p).max())
    eta = 0.6
    no = 0
    for _ in range(steps):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        cm = float(c.max())
        step_f = 2.0 * m * cm
        if step_f < best_f - 1e-12:
            best = p.copy(); best_f = step_f
        active = _active_top(c, min(8, c.size))
        accepted = False
        for trial in range(2):
            if trial == 0:
                q = _pattern_update(p, active, eta)
            else:
                q = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, m))
                q = _normalize_vec(q)
            if q is None: continue
            cc = np.convolve(q, q)
            qf = 2.0 * m * float(cc.max())
            if qf < step_f - 1e-12:
                p = q; step_f = qf; accepted = True; no = 0
                eta = min(1.3, eta * 1.07)
                break
        if not accepted:
            no += 1
            eta *= 0.82
            if no == 30:
                p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, m))
                p = _normalize_vec(p)
                if p is None: break
                no = 0
            if no > 200: break
    return best, best_f
```
`_pattern_update` doesn't use rng (signature can omit). We'll define `_pattern_update(p, active, eta)`.

Normalize:
```
def _normalize_vec(a):
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15: return None
    return a / s
```

`_pattern_update`:
```
def _pattern_update(p, active, eta):
    g = _gradient_from_active(p, active)
    gm = float(g.max())
    if gm <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    g = g / gm
    q = p * np.exp(-eta * g)
    return _normalize_vec(q)
```

Full gradient uses same `_gradient_from_active`, but for full length K=16, active np array. Good.

```

Gradient exact with weights:
```
def _weighted_gradient_fft(a, c, Ngrad, frac):
    n=a.size
    w = np.maximum(c - cmax*(1-frac), 0.0)
    # normalize
    wm = w.max(); if wm>0: w/=wm
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, Ngrad)
    Br = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Ngrad)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(W * Br, Ngrad)
    g = conv[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
    # diagonal correction: for j 0..n-1, add w[2j]*a[j]
    # ensure 2j < len(c)
    idx = np.arange(n)
    w2 = w[:2*n-1]  # len enough
    g += w2[2*idx] * a
    return g
```
But `a[::-1]` is view; rfft okay. `np.arange(n)` allocation each iter O(n). Could precompute `diag_idx = np.arange(2, 2*n, 2)`. In function pass precomputed? To avoid inside, define in propose and use top-level? But helper can allocate; O(n) fine.
Need cmax accessible; pass. If w all zero (flat?), fallback top gradient. If frac=0.3, w for c>0.7max. For all ones triangular, many active around center? c near max maybe few. Good. For random, many. Weighted gradient smooth.
Update `q = a * exp(-eta * g / max(g))`? We can normalize g max1. Add diagonal correction may make g positive. Good.

Does weighted gradient of weighted sum ∑w_i c_i with w≥0 descent? Yes if w positive at high c, reduces them. Normalize. This is like mirror descent. Good.

Let's implement helper:
```
def _weighted_gradient(a, c, Ngrad, frac, diag_idx):
    n = a.size
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    thresh = cmax * (1.0 - frac)
    w = c - thresh
    np.maximum(w, 0.0, out=w)
    wm = float(w.max())
    if wm <= 0:
       active=_active_top(c, min(8, c.size)); return _gradient_from_active(a, active)
    w /= wm
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, Ngrad)
    Br = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Ngrad)
    h = np.fft.irfft(W * Br, Ngrad)[n-1:n-1+n]
    g = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    g += w[diag_idx] * a
    gm=float(g.max())
    if gm >0: g /= gm
    return g
```
`diag_idx = np.arange(0, 2*n-1, 2)` (even indices) length n. `w[diag_idx]` ok.
`a[::-1]` for n=3809 rfft each iter; could precompute? p changes. OK.
Ngrad computed once. Full loop iterations: each has conv FFT (N1) plus gradient FFT (Ngrad two rfft + irfft). More expensive but maybe 10x top slices. Still okay for 200 iter? N=8192/16384, 4 FFTs per iter? conv: rfft+irfft (2), gradient: rfft(w)+rfft(p_rev)+irfft (3) total5. 200*5=1000 FFTs length ~16k, maybe seconds to tens. Could be okay. But if exact time? We'll limit full iters by time. 1100s huge.

We can reduce Ngrad to N1? Need no alias; if N1=8192 <16384, alias corrupts gradient. But maybe using circular correlation with wraparound not too bad? Better correct.

def _try_candidate(seq, N, best_score, best_seq, exact_calls):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(seq).all(): return ...
    seq = np.clip(seq,0,1000)
    s=seq.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return ...
    sur = _surrogate_score(seq, N)
    if not np.isfinite(sur) or sur >= best_score:
       return best_score, best_seq, exact_calls
    sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq]))
    exact_calls += 1
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
       return sc, seq.copy(), exact_calls
    return best_score, best_seq, exact_calls
```

```
def _block_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n=a.size
    q=a.copy()
    for _ in range(5):
        if n<2: break
        width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n//500), max(2, n//20)))
        if width >= n: width=1
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
        q[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
    return _normalize_vec(q)
```

Implementation:
```
def _weighted_gradient(a, c, Ngrad, frac, diag_idx):
    n = a.size
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0: return np.zeros(n)
    w = c - cmax * (1.0 - frac)
    np.maximum(w, 0.0, out=w)
    wm = float(w.max())
    if wm <= 0.0:
        active = _active_top(c, min(8, c.size))
        g = _gradient_from_active(a, active)
    else:
        w /= wm
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, Ngrad)
        Br = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Ngrad)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(W * Br, Ngrad)
        g = conv[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
        g += w[diag_idx] * a
    gm = float(g.max())
    if gm > 0.0:
        g /= gm
    return g
```
`w` is a view of c? `w = c - ...` creates new array 2n-1. `np.maximum` out modifies. Good.
`diag_idx` length n, values 0,2,...,2n-2. `w[diag_idx]` works. `a[::-1]` view.

Full loop using `_weighted_gradient`. We need compute c and cm. `_surrogate_score` recomputes c; avoid by direct in loop. For q score, need qcm; use `_surrogate_score(q,N1)` which recomputes. OK.

When A small, Ngrad maybe large. Good.

while time.monotonic() < full_deadline:
    c = _conv_fft(a_work, N1)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not finite or cm<=0: break
    cur = 2*n*cm
    g = _weighted_gradient(a_work, c, Ngrad, 0.30, diag_idx)
    q = _normalize_vec(a_work * np.exp(-eta * g))
    accepted = False
    if q is not None:
        qscore = _surrogate_score(q, N1)
        if finite and qscore < cur * (1.0 - 1e-7):
            a_work = q; cur = qscore; accepted=True; no=0; improve_streak+=1
            eta = min(1.4, eta * 1.05)
        else:
            # try stronger/ weaker eta?
            q2 = _normalize_vec(a_work * np.exp(-2.0*eta * g))
            if q2 is not None:
                q2score = _surrogate_score(q2, N1)
                if finite and q2score < cur*(1-1e-7): accept...
                else:
                    q2 = _block_perturb(...); check
    if accepted:
       if cur < best_cur - 1e-7: best update; exact if budget
    else:
       q3 = _block_perturb(...)
       if finite and q3score < cur: accept else no++ eta*=0.85; if no>60 strong perturb; if no>400 break
```

def _full_search(start, rng, deadline, n):
    a = _normalize_vec(start)
    if a is None: a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)/float(n)
    N1 = _next_pow2(2*n-1)
    N2 = _next_pow2(3*n-2)
    diag = np.arange(0, 2*n, 2)
    a_work = a.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_cur = _surrogate_score(a, N1)
    eta=0.5; no=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _conv_fft(a_work,N1); cm=c.max(); cur=2*n*cm
        if not finite or cm <=0: break
        g = _weighted_gradient(a_work,c,N2,0.3,diag)
        accepted=False
        if g.max() >0:
            for mult in (1.0, 0.4, 2.0):
                q=_normalize_vec(a_work*np.exp(-eta*mult*g))
                if q is None: continue
                qsc=_surrogate_score(q,N1)
                if finite and qsc < cur*(1-1e-7):
                    a_work=q; cur=qsc; accepted=True; break
        if not accepted:
            q=_block_perturb(a_work, rng, 0.35 + 0.01*min(no,50))
            if q is not None:
                qsc=_surrogate_score(q,N1)
                if finite and qsc < cur:
                    a_work=q; cur=qsc; accepted=True
        if accepted:
            no=0; eta=min(1.5, eta*1.06)
            if cur < best_cur - 1e-8:
                best_cur=cur; best_a=a_work.copy()
        else:
            no +=1; eta*=0.85
            if no > 80:
                a_work=_block_perturb(a_work,rng,1.2)
                if a_work is None: break
                no=0
            if no > 500 or eta < 1e-4: break
    return best_a, best_cur
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def _normalize_vec(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return a / s


def _pattern_objective(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    return float(2.0 * p.size * max(0.0, float(c.max())) / (s * s))


def _build_tile(pattern, n):
    n = int(n)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(pattern)
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return out
    reps = n // m
    if reps > 0:
        out[:reps * m] = np.tile(pattern, reps)
    return out


def _mask_to_pattern(mask, m):
    p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(m):
        if (mask >> i) & 1:
            p[i] = 1.0
    return p


def _binary_search(m, deadline):
    total = 1 << m
    counts = [0] * (2 * m - 1)
    mask = 0
    k = 0
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_mask = (1 << m) - 1

    for step in range(1, total):
        b = (step & -step).bit_length() - 1
        if (mask >> b) & 1:
            k -= 1
            counts[2 * b] -= 1
            mm = mask ^ (1 << b)
            while mm:
                lsb = mm & -mm
                j = lsb.bit_length() - 1
                counts[b + j] -= 2
                mm -= lsb
        else:
            k += 1
            counts[2 * b] += 1
            mm = mask
            while mm:
                lsb = mm & -mm
                j = lsb.bit_length() - 1
                counts[b + j] += 2
                mm -= lsb
        mask ^= (1 << b)

        if k > 0:
            mx = max(counts)
            if mx > 0:
                score = 2.0 * m * mx / (k * k)
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_mask = mask

        if (step & 2047) == 0 and time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

    return best_mask, best_score


def _active_top(c, k):
    n = c.size
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.intp)
    if k >= n:
        return np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    return np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]


def _gradient_from_active(p, active):
    m = p.size
    g = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in active:
        i = int(i)
        jlo = i - m + 1
        if jlo < 0:
            jlo = 0
        jhi = i
        if jhi >= m:
            jhi = m - 1
        if jhi < jlo:
            continue
        g[jlo:jhi + 1] += p[i - jhi:i - jlo + 1][::-1]
        if i % 2 == 0:
            mid = i // 2
            if jlo <= mid <= jhi:
                g[mid] += p[mid]
    return g


def _pattern_update(p, active, eta):
    g = _gradient_from_active(p, active)
    gm = float(g.max())
    if gm <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    g = g / gm
    return _normalize_vec(p * np.exp(-eta * g))


def _optimize_pattern(p, rng, steps, deadline):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return p, float("inf")
    p = p / s
    m = p.size
    best = p.copy()
    best_f = 2.0 * float(m) * float(np.convolve(p, p).max())
    eta = 0.6
    no = 0

    for _ in range(steps):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        cm = float(c.max())
        step_f = 2.0 * m * cm
        if step_f < best_f - 1e-12:
            best = p.copy()
            best_f = step_f

        active = _active_top(c, min(8, c.size))
        accepted = False

        for trial in range(2):
            if trial == 0:
                q = _pattern_update(p, active, eta)
            else:
                q = _normalize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, m)))
            if q is None:
                continue
            cf = 2.0 * m * float(np.convolve(q, q).max())
            if cf < step_f - 1e-12:
                p = q
                step_f = cf
                accepted = True
                no = 0
                eta = min(1.3, eta * 1.07)
                break

        if not accepted:
            no += 1
            eta *= 0.82
            if no == 30:
                p = _normalize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, m)))
                if p is None:
                    break
                no = 0
            if no > 200:
                break

    return best, best_f


def _next_pow2(x):
    n = 1
    while n < x:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _conv_fft(a, N):
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, N)
    return c[:2 * a.size - 1]


def _surrogate_score(a, N):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(_conv_fft(a, N).max())
    if cm < 0.0:
        cm = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * cm / (s * s))


def _try_candidate(a, N, best_score, best_seq):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return best_score, best_seq
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-12:
        return best_score, best_seq
    sur = _surrogate_score(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(sur) or sur >= best_score - 1e-9:
        return best_score, best_seq
    sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return sc, a.copy()
    return best_score, best_seq


def _weighted_gradient(a, c, Ngrad, frac, diag_idx):
    n = a.size
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = c - cmax * (1.0 - frac)
    np.maximum(w, 0.0, out=w)
    wm = float(w.max())
    if wm <= 0.0:
        active = _active_top(c, min(8, c.size))
        g = _gradient_from_active(a, active)
    else:
        w /= wm
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, Ngrad)
        Br = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Ngrad)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(W * Br, Ngrad)
        g = conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n].copy()
        g += w[diag_idx] * a

    gm = float(g.max())
    if gm > 0.0:
        g /= gm
    return g


def _block_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return None
    q = a.copy()
    for _ in range(5):
        lo = max(1, n // 500)
        hi = max(lo + 1, n // 20)
        if hi > n:
            hi = n
        width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
        if width > n:
            width = 1
        hi = n - width + 1
        if hi <= 0:
            continue
        start = int(rng.integers(0, hi))
        q[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
    return _normalize_vec(q)


def _full_search(start, rng, deadline, n):
    a = _normalize_vec(start)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))

    N1 = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    N2 = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    diag = np.arange(0, 2 * n, 2, dtype=np.intp)

    a_work = a.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_cur = _surrogate_score(a, N1)
    eta = 0.5
    no = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _conv_fft(a_work, N1)
        cm = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
            break
        cur = 2.0 * n * cm

        g = _weighted_gradient(a_work, c, N2, 0.30, diag)
        accepted = False
        if g.max() > 0.0:
            for mult in (1.0, 0.4, 2.0):
                q = _normalize_vec(a_work * np.exp(-eta * mult * g))
                if q is None:
                    continue
                qsc = _surrogate_score(q, N1)
                if np.isfinite(qsc) and qsc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-7):
                    a_work = q
                    cur = qsc
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if not accepted:
            q = _block_perturb(a_work, rng, 0.35 + 0.01 * min(no, 50))
            if q is not None:
                qsc = _surrogate_score(q, N1)
                if np.isfinite(qsc) and qsc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-8):
                    a_work = q
                    cur = qsc
                    accepted = True

        if accepted:
            no = 0
            eta = min(1.5, eta * 1.06)
            if cur < best_cur - 1e-8:
                best_cur = cur
                best_a = a_work.copy()
        else:
            no += 1
            eta *= 0.85
            if no > 80:
                nk = _block_perturb(a_work, rng, 1.2)
                if nk is None:
                    break
                a_work = nk
                no = 0
            if no > 500 or eta < 1e-4:
                break

    return best_a, best_cur


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    hard_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)

    fallback = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = np.ones(3809, dtype=np.float64)

    if parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size == 0:
        parent = np.ones(3809, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(parent).all():
        parent = np.ones(parent.size if parent.size > 0 else 3809, dtype=np.float64)
    parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(parent.size)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 3809
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    best_seq = parent.copy()
    best_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq]))
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    N1 = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    base4 = np.array([1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    base2 = np.array([1.0, 0.5], dtype=np.float64)

    for pat in (base4, base2):
        seq = _build_tile(pat, n)
        if seq.size > 0 and seq.sum() > 1e-12:
            best_score, best_seq = _try_candidate(seq, N1, best_score, best_seq)

    if n >= 4 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline:
        m_list = (13, 16, 18, 24, 32, 48, 64)
        pattern_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, min(90.0, 0.12 * float(budget_s)))
        pattern_deadline = min(pattern_deadline, hard_deadline - 30.0)

        for m in m_list:
            if m > n or time.monotonic() >= pattern_deadline:
                continue

            starts = []
            if m <= 18:
                mask, _ = _binary_search(m, pattern_deadline)
                starts.append(_mask_to_pattern(mask, m))

            pb = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            reps4 = m // 4
            if reps4 > 0:
                pb[:reps4 * 4] = np.tile(base4, reps4)
            if pb.sum() <= 0.0:
                pb = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
            starts.append(pb)

            starts.append((rng.random(m) < 0.5).astype(np.float64))
            starts.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) * 20.0))
            starts.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) * 1.0))

            steps = 220 if m <= 16 else (160 if m <= 32 else 100)
            best_p = None
            best_pf = float("inf")

            for st in starts:
                if time.monotonic() >= pattern_deadline:
                    break
                p, pf = _optimize_pattern(st, rng, steps, pattern_deadline)
                if np.isfinite(pf) and pf < best_pf:
                    best_p = p
                    best_pf = pf

            if best_p is not None:
                seq = _build_tile(best_p, n)
                if seq.size > 0 and seq.sum() > 1e-12:
                    best_score, best_seq = _try_candidate(seq, N1, best_score, best_seq)

    if n >= 8 and hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 80.0:
        remain = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        first_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(20.0, remain - 60.0)
        fs_a, _ = _full_search(best_seq, rng, first_deadline, n)
        if fs_a is not None and fs_a.size == n and np.isfinite(fs_a).all():
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fs_a]))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = fs_a.copy()

        if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 35.0:
            uniform = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            mix = _normalize_vec(0.6 * _normalize_vec(best_seq) + 0.4 * uniform)
            if mix is None:
                mix = uniform
            rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
            second_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, rem - 8.0)
            fs2_a, _ = _full_search(mix, rng, second_deadline, n)
            if fs2_a is not None and fs2_a.size == n and np.isfinite(fs2_a).all():
                sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fs2_a]))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = fs2_a.copy()

    final = _normalize_vec(best_seq)
    if final is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final]))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = final
            best_score = sc

    final = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if final.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(final).all():
        final = np.ones(n if n > 0 else 1, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(final.sum()) < 0.02:
        final = np.ones(final.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
